# Finite Particle Grammar, Starbreaker and Binding

## ATOM3D: contact, grammar and signed decoding — 14 September 2026

A3D41-T18-CONTACT-R2 uses SLC-GEN3-R3; A3D41-RXT-R3 supplies the joint native successor. Ordinary contact retains four minima and 113,664 agreeing readouts. Li-6 grammar retains both selected covers and every tie across six placements and 128 rho settings. Two Write responses and a signed N01 bit recover all 192 tested configurations. The test result suggests strong contact with the concept. The separate distinct-selector and physical-coefficient/MeV work remain owner-paused.

[Current derivations, code and results](../../research/atom3d/README.md).

## Retained source-era derivation and results

The following development retains its original experimental context and revision fields. Historical engine selections and campaign status in this source-era account are superseded by the dated current section above.


## Conceptual abstract

This parent follows one object through two volumes. Volume II constructs a
finite matter row only after its source table, schema, address and operator
are fixed. It distinguishes the several finite surfaces by purpose, develops
mass and binding channels, and preserves carriers and containers that are not
ordinary matter. Volume III then serializes those typed objects into a finite
computation grammar. The execution does not flatten them into anonymous
numbers: exact rationals remain exact, non-row operators remain non-row, and
the W8/X1/W9 lifecycle retains its typed direction.

The complete route is

```text
typed matter surface and row grammar
→ current finite-particle catalog
→ mass and binding channels
→ carrier/container return
→ N100 computation grammar
→ SAM Language and exact program semantics
→ W8/X1/W9 lifecycle
→ Theta18 return
→ Starbreaker formation and binding readback
→ ATOM3D matter reception and Theta18 re-emission
```

This parent replaces a stale synthesis by rebuilding the route from the
current source spines, atomic records and exact result artifacts. The older
SAM_321 exemplar may suggest organization; no equation, count, status or
current claim is accepted merely because it appears there.

## Reading map

| Order | Document | Function in the route |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | `SAMA-D000027` | Establish the current finite particle grammar and exact 321-row census. |
| 2 | `SAMA-D000032` | Derive binding and the exact neutron-excess asymmetry bridge. |
| 3 | `SAMA-D000035` | Compile typed source rows into SAM Language without erasing state/history boundaries. |
| 4 | `SAMA-D000039` | Fix the W8/X1/W9 and Theta18 lifecycle that the programs execute. |
| 5 | `SAMA-D000040` | Develop formation and emitted carrier/source-support behavior in Starbreaker. |
| 6 | `SAMA-D000041` | Carry directional history and binding readback without conflating a path with its endpoint. |
| 7 | `SAMA-D000058` | Execute source-native ATOM3D reception, live Starbreaker custody, Theta18 re-emission and informational geometry. |

`SAMA-D000026` supplies the typed row surfaces, `SAMA-D000033` supplies the
carrier/container return and `SAMA-D000034` supplies the N100 grammar. They
are prerequisites because the parent cannot define its cross-volume arrows
by referring forward to their eventual outputs.

## 1. Ontology and type firewall

### 1.1 Matter row

A finite particle row is not identified by one number. Its identity is the
tuple

\[
\mathfrak r=(\mathcal S,\Sigma,a,\mathcal O),
\]

where (mathcal S) is the source table, (Sigma) the row schema, (a) the
typed address and (mathcal O) the acting operator. Partition, sign, charge,
depth, route combination, surface, bin and known label are meaningful only
inside this tuple.

Two rows carrying the same scalar can remain different because their schema
or operator differs. Conversely, one row can appear in more than one derived
predicate without becoming two source rows.

### 1.2 Substrate atom and lifecycle roles

The operational packet distinguishes

- `X1`: transient activator;
- `W8`: octahedral substrate atom; and
- `W9`: completed relation \(X1+W8=W9\).

All three have zero mass lift in this packet. W9 is not a second object added
beside X1 and W8; it names their completed relation. None is automatically a
matter row or tensor.

### 1.3 Carrier, container and ordinary row

Theta18 is released carrier traffic. It has no seated target row and no
retained lift fee. F81 is an aggregate face value. The A-field carrier is
transport infrastructure outside the thirteen-value full-face expression.
These roles are heterogeneous and must survive serialization.

### 1.4 State, program and history

A serialized row set is an input state space. A SAM Language program is a
typed operator on that space. A W8/X1/W9 event is an executed lifecycle. Its
append-only or quotient record is a history. Starbreaker consumes and emits
typed structures through that lifecycle; none of these objects is renamed
into another because one file contains all of them.

## 2. Finite surfaces are purpose-indexed censuses

The source system contains several finite row counts:

| Surface | Count | Question answered |
|---|---:|---|
| compact base mass closure | 35 | Which rows participate in the compact mass-prediction chain? |
| lift table | 139 | Which typed rows carry a registered normalized-lift form? |
| finite catalog | 321 | How are current finite rows and extended objects binned in the catalog schema? |
| matter inventory | 126 | Which 63 stable single writes and 63 bound composites form the fixed inventory? |
| stable tensor-compatible surface | 80 | Which 48 matter and 32 antimatter rows satisfy the structural surface predicate? |
| periodic surface | 126 | Which element-family rows enter the periodic construction? |
| computation basis | 100 | Which physical/source rows enter the executable N100 grammar after the declared exclusions? |

The counts do not compete. They are cardinalities of different typed sets.
An attempt to reconcile them by adding or deleting rows until the numbers
agree would destroy the schemas that make them meaningful.

### 2.1 The 321-row census

CR252 preserves the exact catalog partition

\[
321=63+25+42+169+6+8+8,
\]

with bins

- 63 stable matter;
- 25 unstable resonances;
- 42 antimatter conjugates;
- 169 bound composites;
- 6 carrier-only rows;
- 8 hidden-support rows; and
- 8 rejected fake closures.

Add the terms step by step:

\[
63+25=88,
\quad 88+42=130,
\quad 130+169=299,
\]

\[
299+6=305,
\quad 305+8=313,
\quad 313+8=321.
\]

The arithmetic confirms completeness of that schema. It does not imply that
the 63 stable rows are the same set as the 63 single writes in the separate
126-row inventory; identity must be recovered from each source table.

### 2.2 Downstream reveal boundary

Known particle names, periodic labels, measured masses and external
comparators enter only after the native row is constructed. Where the source
does not authorize an exact known label, the reversible SAM identifier is
retained. This prevents a familiar name or measurement from becoming a hidden
input to the row grammar.

## 3. Full-face cardinality without a fabricated geometry

### 3.1 Typed expression

The owner-supplied ordered role-value expression is

\[
1,1,2,3,4,6,8,8,9,9,12,18,81.
\]

The first twelve values sum to the final face value:

\[
1+1+2+3+4+6+8+8+9+9+12+18=81.
\]

Including the final aggregate yields

\[
81+81=162.
\]

This is a typed role expression, not a list of thirteen homogeneous tensors.
It contains non-tensor lifecycle objects, ordinary mass-lift values, the
zero-rest Theta18 carrier and the F81 aggregate.

### 3.2 Eighty plus one

With \(S=8\) and \(W9=9=S+1\),

\[
\mathcal F=81=9^2.
\]

Factor the stable-surface count:

\[
80=81-1=9^2-1=(9-1)(9+1)=8\cdot10.
\]

Thus

\[
80\ \text{active rows}+1\ \text{non-row closure/contact address}=81.
\]

Doubling the face value gives the terminal ledger \(2\mathcal F=162\).
The extra address is specifically non-row; the identity does not create an
eighty-first matter row.

### 3.3 Preserved failed geometry searches

CR283 retains the cardinality weld while its precommitted punctured
\(9\times9\) and single-field ten-octet searches do not select a row-to-face
geometry. This is a meaningful negative result inside the route. The equality
\(80+1=81\) survives; a specific geometric seating does not follow from the
equality and remains open.

## 4. Carrier and container operator

For an exact address \(h^a d^b\), CR281 classifies the route by explicit
conditions:

\[
\operatorname{role}(a,b,v)=
\begin{cases}
\text{TERMINAL CLOSURE},&v=162,\\
\text{FIXED POINT},&(a,b)=(2,1)\ \text{or}\ a=0,b\ge3,\\
\text{OUTPUT},&a=1,1\le b\le3\ \text{or}\ (a,b)=(0,2),\\
\text{OUT},&\text{otherwise}.
\end{cases}
\]

The resulting value roles are

\[
6,9,18,54\to\text{OUTPUT},
\]

\[
12,27,81\to\text{FIXED POINT},
\qquad
162\to\text{TERMINAL CLOSURE}.
\]

This classification explains why Theta18 is output traffic rather than a
fixed container. Its appearance in the same numeric expression as retained
values does not supply a seated target or lift fee.

The original CR281 run also belongs to the methodology of this route: an
initial lookup used the wrong frozen JSON source for a field. The corrected
runner changed the lookup while retaining the verdict tree, inputs and
controls. The bug-affected attempt and repair remain visible; the correction
does not retroactively erase the first execution.

## 5. Mass, lift and binding remain typed before serialization

### 5.1 Normalized lift by row class

For a lift-table row with native source (K) and surface debit or credit
(X), define

\[
Y=\frac XK.
\]

The row class selects the exact form. Examples include

\[
Y_{\rm color}=\operatorname{sgn}(q)\frac{|q|+D}{R},
\]

\[
Y_{\rm equal}=\frac{D+2}{R(D+1)}=\frac5{48},
\]

and

\[
Y_{\rm OCTET}=\frac{D^2+S}{DS^2}=\frac{17}{192}.
\]

An ordinary unequal pair uses

\[
Y=\operatorname{sgn}(a-b)\frac{|a-b|+D}{R^4},
\]

whereas an OCTET-involved unequal pair replaces \(D\) in the numerator by
\(D^2/R\). A single write has \(Y=0\); a no-depth support row has \(Y=1\).
For the photon carrier \(K=X=0\), \(Y\) is undefined rather than defective.

The formulas demonstrate why one global approximate lift law would be a type
error. The computational serializer must carry the row class along with the
rational value.

### 5.2 Exact asymmetry bridge

The binding child develops the registered coefficient

\[
\kappa=\frac{7117}{768}.
\]

For the declared mass- and substrate-side quantities,

\[
Q_{\rm mass}=4A\kappa,
\qquad
Q_{\rm sub}=8Z\kappa+\frac{N-Z}{8}.
\]

Using \(A=N+Z\), expand the mass side:

\[
Q_{\rm mass}=4(N+Z)\kappa=4N\kappa+4Z\kappa.
\]

Subtract the substrate side:

\[
\begin{aligned}
\Delta Q
&=4N\kappa+4Z\kappa-8Z\kappa-\frac{N-Z}{8}\\
&=4\kappa(N-Z)-\frac{N-Z}{8}\\
&=(N-Z)\left(4\kappa-\frac18\right).
\end{aligned}
\]

Insert \(\kappa=7117/768\):

\[
4\kappa-\frac18
=\frac{7117}{192}-\frac{24}{192}
=\frac{7093}{192}.
\]

Therefore

\[
\boxed{\Delta Q=(N-Z)\frac{7093}{192}}.
\]

Squaring and applying the registered normalization produces the exact
coefficient

\[
\frac{50310649}{1366464}.
\]

The route is preserved symbolically so Volume III receives exact rationals,
not decimals stripped of their origin.

## 6. Matter-to-computation serialization

### 6.1 Interface

Volume III receives typed connectors, carriers, matter and antimatter rows,
lift operators, nuclei, isotope/element rows, closure addresses and compressed
accumulation-source outputs. The serializer carries both a value and its
type:

\[
\operatorname{serialize}(x)=(\operatorname{type}(x),\operatorname{value}(x),
\operatorname{source}(x),\operatorname{operator}(x)).
\]

This is an explanatory representation of the registered contract, not a new
physical equation. It makes explicit why equality of scalar values cannot
merge two objects.

### 6.2 N100 pruning boundary

The computation basis contains 100 physical/source rows after five historical
\(p=9,g=0\) rows are excluded. Three boundaries must remain distinct:

1. A-field exclusion concerns non-row infrastructure.
2. The five-row packet exclusion changes the executable row roster.
3. X1 is a non-row lifecycle activator.

The derived W9 receipts \(X1+W8\) add neither source rows nor independent
objects. Consequently, selecting N100 does not rewrite the frozen 35-, 80-,
126-, 139- or 321-row physical/structural artifacts.

The source-native N100-to-F81 compiler audit preserves those row types and
retains the elementwise F81 join as open. The audit is a retest of the
cross-volume serialization boundary, not evidence that computation pruning
has discovered a new physical census.

## 7. SAM Language and lifecycle execution

SAM Language supplies a typed parser-to-evaluator path. A source program is
parsed under a fixed grammar, converted to the exact internal object, executed
without mutating the source definition, and serialized with state/history
separation.

The lifecycle child fixes the conceptual target before implementation:

```text
X1 activation
→ W8 substrate transaction
→ completed W9 relation
→ Theta18 carrier output or return
→ retained state and exact history receipt
```

Because W9 is the completed relation, counting X1, W8 and W9 as three
independent rows would double-count the event. Because Theta18 is output
traffic, seating it as ordinary matter would change the return operator.

## 8. Starbreaker and directional readback

Starbreaker consumes the typed grammar and lifecycle to model formation,
collapse and emitted structure. The parent does not replace its detailed
operators with a metaphor. Its required handoffs are:

1. the input roster is source-derived and finite;
2. connector, matter, carrier and container tags remain attached;
3. lifecycle events are executed in their declared order;
4. emitted carrier or source-support behavior is distinguished from the
   retained material state; and
5. binding or directional history is read from the executed structure rather
   than imposed as a row label.

The directional-history child then separates endpoint state from the route by
which it was formed. This mirrors Volume I's endpoint/road firewall at a new
technical layer: identical endpoints need not carry identical histories.

## 9. Focused evidence and deviation chain

| Step | Test | Role and preserved outcome | Boundary |
|---:|---|---|---|
| 1 | [`QP:QP093A`](../tests/courtroom/workbench-misc/README.md) | Supplies the 321-row structural bucket map used to organize reconstruction. | Organizational evidence does not authorize stale SAM_321 content. |
| 2 | [`CR:CR252@09a`](../tests/courtroom/09a-particle-mass-chain-cr252-particle-catalog-spine-refresh/README.md) | Rebuilds the 321-row catalog and exact typed bin counts from current sources. | Its bins do not redefine the 80-, 126- or N100 surfaces. |
| 3 | [`CR:CR281@09a`](../tests/courtroom/09a-particle-mass-chain-cr281-carrier-container-functional-operator/README.md) | Executes the carrier/container classifier; the initial source-lookup defect and exact repair remain preserved. | Theta18 remains released traffic rather than fixed matter or fee. |
| 4 | [`CR:CR283@09a`](../tests/courtroom/09a-particle-mass-chain-cr283-eighty-rows-plus-one-closure-address-equals-face-81/README.md) | Retains \(80+1=81\) and \(2\mathcal F=162\); both declared geometry searches fail to select a seating. | Cardinality closes; row-to-face geometry remains open. |
| 5 | `G:G1@SAM-RESEARCH` | Replays source-native N100-to-F81 compiler custody. | Pruning does not rewrite physical tables; elementwise F81 join remains open. |

No route in this parent is assigned a new SAM result classification. Exact
historical status and verdict fields remain available in the generated test
index.

## 10. What is established

The reconstructed route establishes, within its registered scope:

- finite row identity is schema- and operator-dependent;
- the 35-, 80-, 100-, 126-, 139- and 321-row surfaces answer different
  questions;
- the current 321-row catalog retains its exact seven-bin census;
- 80 active rows plus one non-row closure address reach F81 cardinality,
  without supplying a row-to-face geometry;
- carrier/container roles are defined by the exact address operator;
- lift and binding rationals remain typed and exact through serialization;
- N100 pruning changes an executable basis without rewriting physical source
  surfaces; and
- SAM Language, lifecycle, Starbreaker and directional readback receive those
  types rather than inferring them from scalar values.

## 11. Exact open boundaries

1. The punctured \(9\times9\) and ten-octet searches do not select a
   row-to-face geometry.
2. The established contact-to-X1-to-W9 route does not close the separate
   \(A\to B/\text{contact}\) or direct \(A\to X1\) welds.
3. The current Volume II export does not supply a complete chemistry operator.
4. Periodic grammar does not silently become current ATOM3D geometry, binding
   or physical calibration.
5. N100-to-F81 custody does not close an elementwise F81 compiler join merely
   because cardinalities are compatible.
6. A successful exact computation establishes its executed object and
   receipt; physical interpretation remains separately typed.
